## Session and branch metrics

AIPerf exposes AgentX branch/session outcomes through `branch_stats`. These metrics distinguish parent sessions from spawned child branches; they are not the same as completed HTTP requests.

| Configuration | Children completed | Children errored | Children truncated | Parents resumed | Request latency p50 (s) | Request latency p95 (s) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| No offload | 115 | 0 | 16 | 14 | 43.601 | 92.531 |
| CPU 64 GiB | 343 | 0 | 4 | 35 | 21.229 | 58.011 |
| CPU 64 GiB + NVMe | 347 | 0 | 6 | 39 | 16.692 | 45.871 |
| CPU 64 GiB + CephFS | 154 | 0 | 31 | 20 | 12.592 | 153.599 |
| CPU 64 GiB + CephFS (tuned) | 348 | 0 | 2 | 38 | 16.576 | 45.133 |
| CPU 64 GiB + CephFS (tuned, 2) | 373 | 0 | 0 | 48 | 6.890 | 45.278 |

Figure 12 compares completed, errored, and truncated child branches. Provenance: native AIPerf `branch_stats`.
